# Reel / Story Export Review

## Baseline

- Date: 2026-11-03.
- Environment: Windows, Node.js v24.19.0, existing root Jest Node/VM environment.
- Command: `npm test -- --runInBand --runTestsByPath __tests__/export-utils.test.js __tests__/dashboard-export-download.test.js __tests__/dashboard-media.test.js`.
- Result before implementation: 3 suites passed, 293 tests passed, 0 snapshots; 8.234 seconds.
- Existing helpers inspected: export VM `createDownloadRuntime`, `deferredValue`, PNG/decode/save mocks, media injected-document runtime and synthetic ZIP helper.
- Setup verified: Git repository on main; existing ignore patterns protect node_modules, coverage, dist/build, environment files, logs and local private data. No root Docker, ESLint, Prettier, Terraform, Helm or npm-publishing setup requires another ignore file. The API Dockerfile belongs to the unchanged independent service.
- This baseline does not certify the new feature. Specification-quality checklists remain unchanged (16/16).

## Inventory Expansion

T002 will expand the live export/PB/media definitions into case rows covering browser, target/variant, fixture/expected values, filters, period/metric/units/aggregation, density, format, caption state and continuation pages. Every row starts unverified; decoded native-size PNG, phone-size, safe-area, data/privacy/provenance/parity results and reviewer references are required before it can pass.

## Release Disposition

IN PROGRESS. T001-T032 are implemented/tested, including all 13 family paths, PB details/overview, media variants, measured continuation and the user-facing three-format selector. T033 actual-file review remains in progress; US2-US4 and final release gates remain open. No full-inventory image acceptance, completed browser certification or participant result is claimed.

## US1 Implementation and Preliminary File Evidence

- T009-T012 regression cases were demonstrated failing before the corresponding implementation. Owner models and the family registry now satisfy those contracts; there is no generic story screenshot fallback.
- Integrated checkpoint before later image-driven fixes: 24 relevant export/owner/integration suites passed, 902 tests passed. Subsequent targeted export, media, wordcloud and map suites passed after each repair; final T032 rerun is recorded when performed.
- Current integrated T032 rerun after image-driven fixes: 24 suites passed, 908 tests passed, 0 snapshots; 12.309 seconds. Diagnostics report no errors in the 14 changed production modules, markup, or fixture harness.
- Native installed browsers detected: Chrome 156.0.8078.80 and Edge 156.0.4314.58. Chrome was launched with an isolated temporary headless review profile, not the user's regular profile. Edge certification has not been performed.
- First real distance file: 1080 x 1920, all 2013-2025 periods retained over pages, 149728 bytes, SHA-256 a8937bd4135e2d2d7740f7c6ad9b5ab638e19e87fd91f0b95d37458b01cf0d90. Visual review found split year digits and omitted central total; that image is defective, not accepted. Measured year capacity and central-value layout were repaired and regression tested.
- The same file review found the embedded logo text too small; an explicit 36 px TriAnalytica wordmark and separate readable domain now occupy the measured footer, without changing the safe bounds.
- Actual browser family smoke run produced story images for Distance, Pace metrics, Equipment, Equipment Timeline, Distributions, Workout Time, Calendar, Pie Charts, Lifetime Statistics, PB overview, and Map. It found WordCloud's exclusive minSize boundary and media per-page source selection defects; both were reproduced in tests and repaired. Wordcloud then produced a complete story image in the browser. These smoke outcomes are not complete matrix sign-offs.
- A native Standalone Chrome equipment download was read from the local filesystem: private-data/interim/reel077/trianalytica-equipment-20261103-121403-page-1-of-6.png; 178259 bytes, 1080 x 1920, SHA-256 c7efc37ef17bc746f9351c3255dd56ea321c769e96c11ebe87d81989ef173426. Preview and actual file size agree; full native image was inspected. Phone-size/all-page review remains to be recorded.
- Initial native Daily case exposed fixed six-tick date-label overflow. A measured tick-density regression was added and the plotting code repaired without dropping periods; 295 export/utility tests passed after that repair.
- Local environment notes: PowerShell's default localhost request can be routed through the company proxy; direct 127.0.0.1 loopback succeeds. A stale integrated-browser routing registration held a reload; a fresh loopback page worked. Standalone Chrome DevTools is reached by its explicit local WebSocket. Remote Playwright saveAs cannot read the browser host filesystem, so native downloads are directed to local private-data/interim/reel077 and independently verified there. Windows backslash download paths are required; a forward-slash path resulted in a cancelled test download and was not counted as evidence.
- The pre-existing analysis-count endpoint reports local-origin CORS failure; no counter/API code was changed. Fixture imports use sample operations and remain local.
- Completed native Chrome smoke cohort: 15 dense Story/off family/variant cases, 135 actual PNGs, including every family, PB overview/profile and annual/collage outputs. All 135 files were independently read from disk and matched exact native dimensions, byte counts and SHA-256 hashes captured from the approved previews. Verification is stored locally in private-data/interim/reel077/native-verification.json. This proves native byte/dimension parity for that cohort, not complete visual or all-format acceptance.
- Later sparse Story/off cohort: 15 cases, 36 actual PNGs. Real glyph diagnostics found a missing default Y-tick font in Scatter and ambiguous dense Workout Time time-block labels. Regression tests were added and passed after repair; private chart ticks are now always at least 36 px, and two-hour blocks use compact horizontal hours with an explicit block-size axis title.
- Sparse Scatter also showed boundary-clipped bubbles and repeated rounded pace ticks. Export datasets now preserve data coordinates while allowing full edge marks with canvas padding/axis offsets; very narrow pace domains use explicit decimal-unit ticks. Unit tests retain exact original observations.
- Calendar file diagnostics found overlapping three-letter weekday labels in a two-column month. Export weekdays now use unique two-letter labels at 36 px; tests and fresh-browser glyph checks pass. Browser cache was explicitly disabled with CDP Network.setCacheDisabled, and the actual loaded short-label source was checked before the clean rerun.
- Fresh cache-disabled rerun: Scatter and Workout Time Story/off had no glyph-boundary, minimum-font or text-rectangle collision findings; all six sparse calendar pages had none. Wordcloud text rectangles can intersect even when letters interlock without touching; its actual PNG was inspected and rectangle intersections remain manual-review candidates, not automatically failed or passed.
- Image instrumentation version 2 additionally checks partial source-canvas text clipping and rejects stale instrumentation. A fresh sparse square PB overview rendered 29 pages; its first page had no outer/inner text clipping, too-small text or text-rectangle collisions. This is diagnostic evidence, not all-page sign-off.
- PB detail exports now include the actual selected source period/time domain. Distributions and Workout Time use local date formatting rather than UTC day conversion; prior native files with shifted date text are old evidence, not approved current outputs.
- Single-media collage pages now use a full-width column and measured available image height rather than a half-empty two-column template; the selected media set, routes and minimum typography are unchanged.
- The full native Chrome preliminary cohort (all 90 variants, both densities, all three formats, humour off: 540 cases) is in progress. No generated matrix row is pre-approved based on software tests or this progress note.

## Foundation Evidence

- T003: five format/safe-area/typography cases failed before implementation; all 107 export utility tests pass afterward.
- T004/T006: immutable allowlisted snapshots, finite epoch dates, null/zero preservation, separate selection/dataset identities, deep freeze and complete private descriptor registry pass; root dashboard objects remain mutable and isolated.
- T007: explicit frozen chart inputs preserve original data/options/visibility after live mutation; implicit callbacks are rejected without explicit owner-provided export options, inspection can be disabled, and visibility/render failures destroy private instances.
- T008: active media preparation retains sources until completion, rejects new consumers after release, and releases exactly once; partial snapshot attachment failure cleans acquired media plus detached DOM. Prior immediate-cancel expectations were updated to the specified deferred-release contract.
- Integrated command: `npm test -- --runInBand --runTestsByPath __tests__/export-utils.test.js __tests__/dashboard-export-download.test.js __tests__/dashboard-media.test.js __tests__/dashboard-tooltips.test.js __tests__/index-script-syntax.test.js __tests__/tab-navigation.test.js`.
- Result: 6 suites passed, 501 tests passed, 0 snapshots; 1.394 seconds. Current navigation IDs/order and classic-script syntax checks remain green.
- Fresh integrated browser load with script conditional-cache headers removed: 13 adapters, story 1080 x 1920, safe bounds (72,240,888,1320), axis/context/branding 36 px, independent frozen selection value after source mutation, tooltip bridge present.
- Family render functions remain explicitly unregistered pending US1; the private registry throws rather than offering a misleading generic story fallback. Utility support is not full-feature readiness.

## T002 Fixture Evidence

- Fixtures: `__tests__/fixtures/reel-story-exports/` contains the browser/Node generator, import recipes, sparse/dense ZIPs and CSVs, and expected-values.json. No personal data was used.
- Source-derived inventory: 13 target keys, all 15 sport/distance definitions, longest/elevation records, all eight power durations, the power profile, media annual/collage, and metric/group/aggregation variants. The generated matrix below contains 2,160 initially unverified cases.
- Independent Node check: persisted CSVs processed by the real dashboard-utils parser match activity counts and every sport's expected distance/moving-time totals.
- Browser import environment: VS Code integrated browser, Windows, Electron 43.7.7 / Chromium 150.0.7871.250. This is fixture-import evidence, not certification of current stable standalone Chrome or Edge.
- Both saved ZIPs were fetched from the static server and passed through real importStravaZip followed by importCsvText/applyImportedDataset; both CSV-only recipes were separately processed through importCsvText. The visible Strava dialog only accepts ZIP; CSV-only uses the local harness.
- Sparse ZIP: 15 activities, 3 GPX tracks, one actual MP4; each of the eight power durations has one record at 200 W.
- Dense ZIP: 195 activities across 2013-2025, 39 GPX tracks, 13 FIT power histories; each duration has 13 records improving from 200 to 296 W. The media library recognizes 13 JPEGs and one MP4 as readable.
- JPEG decoded at 480 x 320 pixels. Actual MP4 decoded through createVideoPreview to an image data URL for both recipes. Container bytes came from the browser's MP4 encoder, not a renamed WebM or dummy file.
- Harness provenance: beginImportOperation('sample') avoids test-analysis counting; onDatasetPrepared sets sourceType='sample' and sampleProvenance.synthetic=true before application. CSV-only correctly has no tracks or media/power capability; full-family review uses the ZIPs.
- Fixture correction during actual review: the existing name-based equipment heuristic initially classified generic synthetic shoe names as bikes. Regenerated inputs now use fictional Nike review-pair names; real browser imports confirm 13 Shoes and 13 Bikes for the dense timeline. Metric totals and FIT/GPX/media data are unchanged. Expected media order/default selection now follows the existing newest-first stable chronology, independently checked against the imported media library; the default dense twelve already include its video.
- Browser download-event limitation: fixture bytes were passed locally to the Node generator to save; this does not demonstrate or modify application image-download behaviour.
- Actual exported PNG review, narrow viewport checks, visual parity, standalone Chrome/Edge acceptance and human review remain open.
